       63 -### Configuration (`src/config/`)                             
       64 -- `manager.py` is the canonical entry — it loads three TOMLs a
          -t import time (`paths.toml`, `settings.toml`, `models.toml`), 
          -exposes `DIRS`, `SEED`, `MULTI_LABEL_COLS`, `PROJECT_ROOT`, an
          -d `get_model_config(model_name)`. It validates everything agai
          -nst `ConfigValidator` (currently in the deleted `src/utils/val
          -idation.py` — see refactor note).                             
       65 -- `loader.py` defines an alternative `ModelConfigLoader` class
          - but `manager.get_model_config` is what the rest of the code c
          -alls.                                                         
       66 -- Model definitions live in `src/config/models.toml`. Each mod
          -el (e.g. `TwoTowerGAT`) declares `cols`, `features`, `targets`
          -, dimension hints, and two parameter blocks: `[Model.params]` 
          -(active hyperparameters) and `[Model.optuna]` (search space, w
          -ith values like `["log", 1e-4, 1e-2]`, `["categorical", 64, 12
          -8]`, `["int", 4, 6]`).                                        
       89 +## Conventions to keep                                        
       90  
       68 -### Training Path (`src/pipeline.py` → `src/modeling/engine/`)
       69 -`train_pipeline` orchestrates: load+validate config → load+val
          -idate data → split → estimate memory → build `DoubleTowerDatas
          -et` (validation reuses train's `encoders` — critical) → infer 
          -real `drug_dim`/`haplo_dim`/`target_dims` from a sample → buil
          -d `DoubleTowerCollater` DataLoaders → `create_gnn_model(...)` 
          -→ `PGenTrainer.fit(...)`. Optuna goes through `src/modeling/en
          -gine/tuner.py::run_optuna_study`, and `PGenTrainer` has an `fr
          -om_optuna` mode that disables checkpointing/compilation.      
       91 +- **Pydantic everywhere at boundaries.** New code accepting ex
          +ternal input (HTTP, CLI args, TSV rows, TOML) goes through `sr
          +c/domain/` or `src/config/` models — `dict[str, Any]` is banne
          +d in those layers.                                            
       92 +- **Coordinates are 1-based.** `Position.pos` matches FASTA/VC
          +F conventions. Anything 0-based is internal-only and named `_0
          +based`.                                                       
       93 +- **Genome build is part of every Position.** Build mismatch i
          +s a fail-fast error (`BioinformaticsError`), not a warning. Se
          +e `iter_variants` for the pattern.                            
       94 +- **Star alleles come from `data/dicts/star_alleles.tsv`.** Do
          +n't reintroduce hardcoded tables in code. Add a row to the TSV
          + instead.                                                     
       95 +- **No `shell=True` in subprocess calls.** Use argv lists. For
          + pipes, use `subprocess.Popen` plumbing (see `ngs_pipeline.Map
          +pingAlignmentAnalysis.map_reads`).                            
       96 +- **No `input()` in library code.** Interactive prompts live i
          +n `src/interface/cli.py` or future `src/cli/workflows/`. Libra
          +ry functions take parameters or return generators.            
       97 +- **Use `get_settings()` / `get_model_config()` in new code**,
          + not `from src.config.manager import DIRS, SEED, …`. The shim 
          +exists for legacy callers and will be removed in a follow-up p
          +hase.                                                         
       98 +- **No emoji in log messages.** Emoji are for `ConsoleIO` user
          +-facing output. Log messages use `logger.info("Doing X (sample
          +=%s)", sample_id)` style.                                     
       99 +- **English everywhere.** Spanish identifiers/comments in `src
          +/` are tech debt to fix; `BACKUPS/` is exempted.              
      100  
       71 -### Data (`src/data/`)                                        
       72 -- `datasets.py` — `DoubleTowerDataset` returns dicts with `dru
          -g_data` and `haplo_data` PyG `Data` objects.                  
       73 -- `collator.py` — `DoubleTowerCollater` batches the two towers
          - into a single batch.                                         
       74 -- `lib_builder_polars.py` / `lib_builder_v2.py` — graph-librar
          -y builders (drugs from SMILES, variants from VCF/TSV against `
          -data/ref_genome/HSapiens_GChr38.fa`). See `docs/LIBRARY_BUILDE
          -R.md` for input schemas.                                      
      101 +## Outstanding tech debt                                      
      102  
       76 -### Genomics (`src/genomics/`)                                
       77 -VCF handling, reference genome wrappers, and variant validatio
          -n. Variants must be **1-based**; `start_pos` is decremented in
          -ternally before FASTA lookup. Mismatches typically come from c
          -hromosome naming (`chr1` vs `NC_000001`) — `GLOBAL_CHROM_MAPPI
          -NG` handles this.                                             
       78 -                                                              
       79 -### Interface (`src/interface/`)                              
       80 -- `cli.py` — interactive menu (`main_menu_loop`), uses lazy im
          -ports (`_get_train_pipeline`, etc.) to keep startup fast.     
       81 -- `ui.py` — `ConsoleIO`, `Spinner`, `ProgressBar` primitives. 
          -Use these instead of bare `print` for user-facing output.     
       82 -- `io.py` — license/notice printing.                          
       83 -                                                              
       84 -### Outputs / Artifacts                                       
       85 -Everything is rooted at `PROJECT_ROOT` (3 levels up from `src/
          -config/manager.py`). Models save to `src/pgen_model/models/`, 
          -encoders to `src/pgen_model/encoders/`, Optuna reports to `rep
          -orts/optuna_reports/`, training reports to `reports/train_repo
          -rts/`, logs to `logs/`. These are auto-created on import of `m
          -anager.py`.                                                   
       86 -                                                              
       87 -## Conventions                                                
       88 -                                                              
       89 -- New columns/targets must be added to **both** `models.toml` 
          -(`cols`, `features`, `targets`) **and** the relevant dataset/e
          -ncoder logic — `train_dataset.encoders` is reused by validatio
          -n, so anything missing there breaks dimension inference at `_i
          -nfer_dimensions`.                                             
       90 -- For multi-label targets, append the column name to `[project
          -].multi_label_cols` in `settings.toml`; it flows through as `M
          -ULTI_LABEL_COLS`.                                             
       91 -- Errors raised from training/data/config code should use the 
          -typed exception hierarchy in `src/utils/exceptions.py` (`Confi
          -gurationError`, `DataError`, `ModelError`, `MemoryError`/`Phar
          -magenMemoryError`, etc.) rather than bare `ValueError`/`Runtim
          -eError`.                                                      
       92 -- Data files (`data/`, `train_data/`, `src/library/`, model ar
          -tifacts) are gitignored and excluded from AI context via `.aie
          -xclude` — don't expect them to be readable in fresh checkouts.
       93 -- Don't edit anything under `src/CAJON DE SASTRE/` — it's a sc
          -ratch directory.

● Clean. Final tally:

  Phase 4.5 complete.

  - New package: src/data/library/ — 9 files (builder, config, chromosome,
  drugs, genes, pgx, manifest, organize, __main__).
  - Removed: src/data/lib_builder_polars.py (883 LOC),
  src/data/lib_builder_v2.py (broken stub), and the orphaned src/types/ dir.
  - Tests: 47 new under tests/unit/data/test_library_*.py (schema dimensions,
  filename safety, manifest atomicity, UGT1A merging, rsID→star-allele
  resolution from the catalog). 173 total tests passing.
  - CLI: python -m src.data.library --help works; --only-gene, --skip-drugs,
  --skip-genes, --force flags supported.
  - Consumer contract preserved: GraphIndexBuilder still indexes the existing
  5105 drug graphs and 2353 gene variants without changes.

  Key fixes from the old script:
  - No module-level globals (GLOBAL_GENOME gone); FASTA handle is now a
  constructor arg.
  - Paths flow from get_settings().paths, not hardcoded BASE_DIR = Path("data").
  - Resume support via build_manifest.json (atomic write) — critical for limited
   compute.
  - Pure-Python file organization (no bash/PowerShell).
  - data/dicts/star_alleles.tsv is now the single source for rsID→star-allele
  resolution (replacing the hardcoded DPYD_RS_MAP).
  - All Spanish translated; print() → logger.
  - Pydantic LibraryBuildConfig validates inputs at construction.

  Schema dimensions are pinned in tests (25 drug node features, 7 drug edge, 9
  gene node, 3 gene edge) so a future change that would silently invalidate
  trained models will fail CI instead.

  Phase 4 (god-object decomposition of DoubleTowerDataset/PGenTrainer) is the
  natural next step. Ready to continue?

      206 +def _validate_input_dims(dims: dict[str, dict[str, int]]) -> N
          +one:                                                          
      207 +    required_subkeys = ("features", "edges", "attrs")         
      208 +    for kind in ("drugs", "geno"):                            
      209 +        if kind not in dims:                                  
      210 +            continue                                          
      211 +        if not isinstance(dims[kind], dict):

          +pe(dims[kind]).__name__}"                                     
      213 +            raise DataError(msg)

      215 +            if subkey not in dims[kind]:                      
      216 +                continue

      219 +                msg = f"invalid dimension {kind}.{subkey}: {v}
          + (must be non-negative int)"

  4b — DataLoaderUtils split (commit ahead):
  - src/data/loaders.py::TabularLoader — CSV/TSV reader with TRAIN_DATA_SCHEMA +
   unified null tokens. Switched to schema_overrides= so unexpected columns no
  longer crash.

  Stratifier.

  rsID→star-allele lookup now reads data/dicts/star_alleles.tsv instead of the
  hardcoded copy.

  +26 tests.

  4c — DoubleTowerDataset decomposition:
  - src/data/cache.py::GraphCache — RAM cache, on-disk fallback, dummy-graph
  generator. Typed GraphDims replaces the nested-dict spec.
  - src/data/encoders.py::TargetEncoder — fit/transform sklearn encoders for
  single + multi-label targets, reusable across train/val/test.
  - src/data/datasets.py::DoubleTowerDataset shrinks from 820 → 330 lines and
  just orchestrates. Backward-compat preserved: .encoders property, geno_key
  auto-detection. +20 tests. Both pre-existing dataset test failures now fixed.

  4d — PGenTrainer split:
  - src/modeling/training/loop.py::TrainingLoop (ABC) — shared input validation,
   criterion setup, _compute_step, train_epoch, validate, _step_scheduler,
  _check_nan. Subclass hooks for compilation, batch-iter wrapping, epoch-end
  behavior.
  - src/modeling/training/standard.py::StandardTrainer — torch.compile +
  CheckpointManager + tqdm + verbose logging. NaN → TrainingError.
  - src/modeling/training/optuna_trainer.py::OptunaTrialTrainer — accepts a
  Trial kwarg, reports per-epoch val_loss, raises TrialPruned on NaN or pruning.
   No checkpointing or compilation.
  - src/modeling/engine/trainer.py::PGenTrainer is now a 70-line factory
  function dispatching by from_optuna. +14 tests including the contract that
  OptunaTrialTrainer must not carry a checkpoint_manager attribute.

  End-of-Phase-4 system state: 6 entry points still import (pipeline, tuner,
  predictor, trainer, genomics.*, api.main); CLI + API + library builder all
  functional. Only Phase 8 (CI workflow, README/ARCHITECTURE doc sweep) remains
  from the original plan.

       30 -This script intends to build graphs for drugs and genes from *
          -*variant files** ***(.vcf)*** and compound tables.            
       33 +**Schema** (frozen — must stay in sync with the trained TwoTow
          +erGAT model):                                                 
       34  
       32 -## *REQUIREMENTS*                                             
       35 +| Graph kind | Node features | Edge features |                
       36 +| ---------- | ------------- | ------------- |                
       37 +| Drug       | 25            | 7             |                
       38 +| Gene       | 9             | 3             |                
       39  
       34 -### 1. Libs                                                   
       40 +The dimensions live in `src/data/library/{drugs,genes}.py` as 
          +module-level constants and are pinned by `tests/unit/data/test
          +_library_{drugs,genes}.py` so accidental changes fail CI befor
          +e silently invalidating every trained model.                  
       41  
       36 -- ``polars``: High-performance data processing and vectorized 
          -validation.                                                   
       37 -- ``torch`` & ``torch_geometric``: Tensor construction and Dat
          -a objects (graphs).                                           
       38 -- ``rdkit``: Computational chemistry (required for DrugGraphBu
          -ilder).                                                       
       39 -- ``networkx``: Intermediate graph topology construction (node
          -s and edges).                                                 
       40 -- ``pyfaidx``: Fast indexed access to massive FASTA sequences.
          -                                                              
       41 -- ``tqdm``: Progress bar visualization.                       
       42 +---                                                           
       43  
       43 -### 2. Variants FILE (TSV)                                    
       44 +## Inputs                                                     
       45  
       45 -The snp_data_output.tsv file must strictly adhere to the follo
          -wing schema:                                                  
       46 +### 1. Variants TSV (`data/snp_data_output.tsv`)              
       47  
       47 -| Column | Type | Description |                               
       48 -| :---- | :---- | :---- |                                     
       49 -| chr | str | Chromosome identifier (e.g., "1", "X", "chr1"). 
          -Must match FASTA headers. |                                   
       50 -| start_pos | int | Absolute genomic position (**1-based** for
          -mat). |                                                       
       51 -| Ref_Allele | str | Reference allele (must match the FASTA se
          -quence exactly). |                                            
       52 -| Alt_Allele | str | Alternative allele. |                    
       53 -| gene | str | (Optional) Associated gene name for grouping ou
          -tput graphs. |                                                
       54 -| variant_type | str | (Optional) Explicit type: SNP, MNP, INS
          -, DEL, STAR_ALLELE. |                                         
       55 -| FXN_CLASS | str | (Optional) Functional classification (e.g.
          -, "missense_variant", "intron_variant"). |                    
       48 +| Column | Type | Required | Notes |                          
       49 +| ------ | ---- | :---: | ----- |                             
       50 +| `chr` | str | ✓ | `1`, `chr1`, `X`, `NC_000001.11` — all nor
          +malized internally. |                                         
       51 +| `start_pos` | int | ✓ | **1-based** genomic position. |     
       52 +| `Ref_Allele` | str | ✓ | Reference allele; validated against
          + the FASTA. Mismatch → row skipped + warning. |               
       53 +| `Alt_Allele` | str | ✓ | Alternate allele. |                
       54 +| `gene` | str |   | Used to group output graphs into per-gene
          + subdirs. Defaults to "Intergenic" if missing. |              
       55 +| `snp` | str |   | rsID or variant name; falls back to `var_<
          +chrom>_<pos>` if missing. |                                   
       56 +| `variant_type` | str |   | `snv`, `del`, `ins`, `del`, `mnp`
          +, … inferred from REF/ALT lengths if absent. |                
       57 +| `FXN_CLASS` | str |   | Comma-joined functional terms. Used 
          +to set the `is_coding`/`is_regulatory`/`is_splicing`/`is_inter
          +genic` node features. |                                       
       58  
       57 -### 3. Drugs FILE (TSV)                                       
       59 +### 2. Drugs TSV (`data/drugs_cid.tsv`)                       
       60  
       59 -The drugs_cid.tsv file is the source for molecular graphs:    
       61 +| Column | Type | Required | Notes |                          
       62 +| ------ | ---- | :---: | ----- |                             
       63 +| `cid` | int / str | ✓ | PubChem Compound ID. Becomes the fil
          +ename prefix. |                                               
       64 +| `smiles` | str | ✓ | Canonical SMILES; must parse via RDKit.
          + |                                                            
       65 +| `cmpd_name_cleaned` | str | ✓ | Display name. Filesystem-uns
          +afe characters get replaced with `_` before being baked into t
          +he filename. |                                                
       66  
       61 -| Column | Type | Description |                               
       62 -| :---- | :---- | :---- |                                     
       63 -| cid | str/int | Unique compound identifier (e.g., PubChem CI
          -D). |                                                         
       64 -| smiles | str | Valid SMILES string for the compound (e.g., C
          -C(=O)Oc1ccccc1C(=O)O). |                                      
       65 -| cmpd_name_cleaned | str | Cleaned drug name (used for naming
          - the .pt output file). |                                      
       67 +### 3. Reference FASTA (`data/ref_genome/HSapiens_GChr38.fa`) 
       68  
       67 -## ---                                                        
       69 +Indexed (`*.fai`) — needed for the variant validator. If you d
          +on't have one yet:                                            
       70  
       69 -**USAGE INSTRUCTIONS**                                        
       71 +```bash                                                       
       72 +python -c "from src.genomics.ref_genome import ReferenceGenome
          +Manager; ReferenceGenomeManager().run()"                      
       73 +```                                                           
       74  
       71 -### **Basic Execution**                                       
       75 +…will download GRCh38 from Ensembl, decompress, and run `samto
          +ols faidx` + `bwa index`.                                     
       76  
       73 -The script is designed to run from the project root. It automa
          -tically detects the operating system (Debian/Linux or Windows)
          - to handle folder organization scripts.                       
       77 +### 4. Per-gene VCF folder (`data/haplotype_variants/`)       
       78  
       75 -***Any OS***                                                  
       79 +```                                                           
       80 +data/haplotype_variants/                                      
       81 +├── CYP2D6/                                                   
       82 +│   ├── CYP2D6_4.vcf       → labelled  *4                     
       83 +│   ├── CYP2D6_10.vcf      → labelled  *10                    
       84 +│   └── …                                                     
       85 +├── DPYD/                                                     
       86 +│   ├── rs3918290.vcf      → resolved via data/dicts/star_alle
          +les.tsv → *2A                                                 
       87 +│   └── …                                                     
       88 +└── …                                                         
       89 +```                                                           
       90  
       91 +Filename → haplotype label rules:                             
       92 +                                                              
       93 +* `GENE_<n>` → `*<n>`        (digits become star alleles)     
       94 +* `<n>` → `*<n>`             (bare digits)                    
       95 +* `rsXXXX` → looked up in `data/dicts/star_alleles.tsv`. Falls
          + back to the rsID itself when not in the catalog.             
       96 +* `c.2846A>T` and similar HGVS strings are kept verbatim.     
       97 +

      110 -| Error / Symptom | Probable Cause | Solution |               
      111 -| :---- | :---- | :---- |                                     
      112 -| **Chr missing: X** | The chromosome name in the TSV does not
          - match the FASTA file header. | Check if your FASTA uses chr1 
          -or NC_000001 nomenclature. The script uses GLOBAL_CHROM_MAPPIN
          -G to adjust this. |                                           
      113 -| **Ref Mismatch** | The reference base in the TSV is not equa
          -l to the genome at that position. | Ensure start_pos is **1-ba
          -sed**. The script subtracts 1 internally. Verify you are using
          - the correct genome build (GRCh38 vs hg19). |                 
      114 -| **Invalid POS format** | Non-numeric values in start_pos. | 
          -Clean the TSV of "N/A", "-", or dots in position columns. |   
      115 -| **Missing Drug Graphs** | RDKit failed to parse the SMILES s
          -tring. | Check the drug_generation_errors.log file generated i
          -n the root directory to see which CIDs failed. |              
      116 -| **Permissions (Linux/Debian)** | Error executing organize_ge
          -nes.sh. | Ensure you have write permissions in the output fold
          -er or manually run chmod +x if the Python script fails to assi
          -gn permissions. |                                             
      155 +For per-piece access:                                         
      156 +                                                              
      157 +```python                                                     
      158 +from src.data.library.drugs import DrugGraphBuilder, smiles_to
          +_graph                                                        
      159 +from src.data.library.genes import GenomicGraphBuilder        
      160 +from src.data.library.manifest import BuildManifest           
      161 +```                                                           
      162 +                                                              
      163 +---                                                           
      164 +                                                              
      165 +## Resume support                                             
      166 +                                                              
      167 +Every successful save updates `src/library/build_manifest.json
          +`. The next run reads it on startup, so:                      
      168 +                                                              
      169 +* An interrupted build (Ctrl-C, kernel OOM, power loss) just r
          +esumes — no rebuilding the first 4000 drugs you already had.  
      170 +* Failures are recorded too: ``manifest.failed["drug:2244"] = 
          +"Invalid SMILES"`` so you can grep the JSON to triage.        
      171 +* The manifest is written atomically (write-temp-then-rename) 
          +so a crash mid-write leaves the previous version intact.      
      172 +                                                              
      173 +To start fresh: `--force`, or delete the manifest manually.   
      174 +                                                              
      175 +---                                                           
      176 +                                                              
      177 +## Troubleshooting                                            
      178 +                                                              
      179 +| Symptom | Cause | Fix |                                     
      180 +| ------- | ----- | --- |                                     
      181 +| `FASTA not found: …/HSapiens_GChr38.fa` | Reference genome n
          +ot downloaded yet. | Run `ReferenceGenomeManager().run()` (see
          + § Inputs). |                                                 
      182 +| `Chromosome 'X' not in FASTA` | TSV chromosome label doesn't
          + appear in FASTA index. Most often happens with mixed `chr1` /
          + `1` / `NC_000001.11` conventions. | The validator already nor
          +malizes; if you still see this, check `pyfaidx` indexed the FA
          +STA correctly. |                                              
      183 +| `REF mismatch: TSV=A vs FASTA=G` | Wrong genome build (GRCh3
          +7 vs GRCh38) or the TSV's `start_pos` is 0-based instead of 1-
          +based. | Confirm the build; remember `start_pos` is **1-based*
          +*. |                                                          
      184 +| `Invalid SMILES` for many drugs | TSV contains non-canonical
          + SMILES strings or salts/mixtures. | Pre-canonicalize via RDKi
          +t before feeding the builder. Failures land in `<library_root>
          +/build_failures.log`. |                                       
      185 +| Drug graphs have 24 features instead of 25 | Old artifacts p
          +roduced before the schema was finalized. | `python -m src.data
          +.library --force` (or selectively delete `src/library/drugs/` 
          +and rerun). |                                                 
      186 +| Gene subdirs are empty after a run | The PGx folder schema d
          +idn't match. | `--verbose` to see which VCFs got parsed; check
          + filenames match the rules in § Inputs. |                     
      187 +| Need only one gene's graphs for testing | | `--only-gene CYP
          +2D6 --skip-drugs` |                                           
      188 +                                                              
      189 +---                                                           
      190 +                                                              
      191 +## What changed vs the old `library_creator_polars.py`        
      192 +                                                              
      193 +The previous single-file 883-line script lives in `BACKUPS/caj
          +on_de_sastre_pre_refactor/`. The rewrite (Phase 4.5):         
      194 +                                                              
      195 +* Removed module-level globals (`GLOBAL_GENOME`, `GLOBAL_CHROM
          +_MAPPING`, …).                                                
      196 +* Replaced hardcoded `BASE_DIR = Path("data")` with `Settings.
          +paths`.                                                       
      197 +* Replaced bash/PowerShell organize scripts with pure Python (
          +`pathlib.Path.rename`).                                       
      198 +* Single source of truth for rsID→star-allele lookups: `data/d
          +icts/star_alleles.tsv`.                                       
      199 +* Added the `build_manifest.json` resume layer.               
      200 +* Translated all Spanish comments + docstrings.               
      201 +* Pydantic-validated build config; CLI built on `argparse` (no
          + custom `--help` menu).                                       
      202 +* 47 unit tests covering schema dimensions, filename safety, m
          +anifest atomicity, UGT1A merging, and rsID resolution.

        5  # 💊 Pharmagen {#eng}
        6  
        7  Author: Adrim Hamed Outmani (@Aderfi)
        8  
        8 -![Python Version](https://img.shields.io/badge/python-3.10-blu
          -e.svg)                                                        
        9 +![Python Version](https://img.shields.io/badge/python-3.14-blu
          +e.svg)                                                        
       10  ![PyTorch](https://img.shields.io/badge/PyTorch-2.0+-ee4c2c.sv
           g)
       11 +![FastAPI](https://img.shields.io/badge/FastAPI-0.135+-009688.
          +svg)                                                          
       12 +![Pydantic](https://img.shields.io/badge/Pydantic-v2-e92063.sv
          +g)                                                            
       13  ![Status](https://img.shields.io/badge/status-Beta-orange.svg)
       14  
       15  > **Pharmacogenetic Prediction and Therapeutic Efficacy via De
           ep Learning.**
       16  
       14 -**Pharmagen** is an advanced bioinformatics software suite des
          -igned to predict phenotypic outcomes, direction of effect, and
          - adverse drug reaction (ADR) types based on a patient's genomi
          -c profile (Gene/Allele) and prescribed medications.           
       17 +**Pharmagen** is a bioinformatics suite that maps a patient's 
          +genomic profile (gene/allele) and prescribed medications to ph
          +enotypic outcomes, direction-of-effect, and adverse-drug-react
          +ion types.                                                    
       18  
       16 -The system's core utilizes a customized **DeepFM (Deep Factori
          -zation Machines)** architecture, which combines the deep learn
          -ing capabilities of Transformers with the efficiency of Factor
          -ization Machines in capturing sparse variable interactions.   
       19 +The active architecture is a **Two-Tower Graph Neural Network*
          +* built on **GATv2** (PyTorch Geometric) — a drug tower over m
          +olecular SMILES graphs, a genotype tower over variant-topology
          + graphs, fused into multi-task heads. (The earlier DeepFM desi
          +gn is being phased out.)                                      
       20  
       21  ---
       22  
       20 -## 🚀 Key Features                                            
       23 +## 🚀 Key features                                            
       24  
       22 -- **Hybrid Architecture:** ~~DeepFM model featuring dynamic **
          -embeddings** and **attention mechanisms** to capture complex d
          -rug-gene interactions.~~                                      
       23 -  Pharmagen was categorical model. **Now working on a GNN stru
          -cture based on GATv2**                                        
       25 +- **Two-Tower GATv2 GNN** — drug-molecule + genotype graphs en
          +coded with attention; multi-task prediction heads for phenotyp
          +e category, direction of effect, and ADR.                     
       26 +- **Offline graph library** — all drug + variant graphs are bu
          +ilt once and stored to disk (`src/library/`); training and inf
          +erence lazy-load. Critical for limited-compute environments.  
       27 +- **FastAPI inference service** — typed Pydantic request/respo
          +nse, OpenAPI docs at `/docs`, health/ready probes, lazy model 
          +loading.                                                      
       28 +- **Optuna integration** — hyperparameter search with per-tria
          +l pruning and a discriminated `OptunaSpec` union (categorical 
          +/ int / float / log) parsed from `models.toml`.               
       29 +- **Pydantic v2 throughout** — domain models (`Drug`, `Variant
          +`, `StarAllele`, `Genotype`, `PredictionRequest`, …) at every 
          +boundary; configuration via `pydantic-settings`.              
       30 +- **NGS pipeline** — FastQC → BWA-MEM → Picard → Freebayes → V
          +EP, no `shell=True`, sane subprocess plumbing.                
       31  
       25 -- **Flexible Inference:**                                     
       26 -  - **Interactive Mode (CLI):** Optimized for rapid, individua
          -l queries.                                                    
       27 -  - **Batch Mode:** For processing large patient datasets via 
          -CSV/TSV files.                                                
       28 -- **Automated Optimization:** Native integration with **Optuna
          -** for automated hyperparameter tuning and framework optimizat
          -ion.                                                          
       29 -- **Modular Configuration:** A TOML-based system (`models.toml
          -`, `config.toml`) that allows users to define new architecture
          -s and parameters without modifying the core source code.      
       30 -- **Bioinformatics Pipeline:** End-to-end processing from geno
          -mic sequencing data (VCF files) and ATC code mapping to final 
          -clinical prediction.

       47  ## 📋 Prerequisites
       48  
       36 -- **Operating System:** Developed on **Debian 13 (Trixie)** an
          -d **Windows 11**.                                             
       37 -- **Python:** Version **3.10** (Strictly recommended).        
       38 -- **Virtual Environment:** Use of `venv` or `conda` is highly 
          -encouraged ****(preferably `venv` through `UV`)****. if `conda
          -` --> `mamba`                                                 
       39 -- **Hardware Requirements:**                                  
       40 -    * [CPU/GPU/RAM requirements]                              
       49 +- **OS:** Linux (developed on Debian 13). Windows works with W
          +SL2; macOS is untested.                                       
       50 +- **Python:** **3.14** (pinned in `.python-version`). The proj
          +ect is managed with [`uv`](https://github.com/astral-sh/uv); `
          +uv.lock` is the source of truth.                              
       51 +- **GPU:** CUDA-capable card recommended; CPU works for infere
          +nce and small training runs.                                  
       52 +- **External tools** (only needed for the NGS pipeline): `samt
          +ools`, `bwa`, `fastp`, `fastqc`, `picard`, `freebayes`, `vcfto
          +ols`, `vep`.

      119 +> **Predicción farmacogenética y eficacia terapéutica mediante
          + Deep Learning.**                                             
      120  
       75 -> **Predicción Farmacogenética y Eficacia Terapéutica mediante
          - Deep Learning.**                                             
      121 +**Pharmagen** es una suite bioinformática que mapea el perfil 
          +genómico del paciente (gen/alelo) y las medicaciones prescrita
          +s a resultados fenotípicos, dirección del efecto y tipos de re
          +acciones adversas.                                            
      122  
       77 -**Pharmagen** es un software avanzado de bioinformática diseña
          -do para predecir resultados fenotípicos, dirección del efecto 
          -y tipos de efectos adversos en pacientes basándose en su perfi
          -l genómico (Gen/Alelo) y fármacos prescritos.                 
      123 +La arquitectura activa es una **Red Neuronal de Grafos Two-Tow
          +er** basada en **GATv2** (PyTorch Geometric) — torre de fármac
          +o sobre grafos moleculares SMILES, torre de genotipo sobre gra
          +fos de topología de variantes, fusionadas en cabezas multi-tar
          +ea. (El diseño DeepFM previo está siendo retirado.)           
      124  
       79 -El núcleo del sistema utiliza una arquitectura **DeepFM (Deep 
          -Factorization Machines)** personalizada, que combina la capaci
          -dad de aprendizaje profundo de los Transformers con la eficien
          -cia en interacciones de variables dispersas de las Máquinas de
          - Factorización.                                               
      125 +---                                                           
      126  
      127 +## 🚀 Características principales                             
      128  
       82 -## 🚀 Características Principales                             
      129 +- **GNN Two-Tower con GATv2** — grafos fármaco-molécula + geno
          +tipo codificados con atención; cabezas de predicción multi-tar
          +ea.                                                           
      130 +- **Librería de grafos offline** — todos los grafos de fármaco
          +s y variantes se construyen una vez y se almacenan en disco (`
          +src/library/`); entrenamiento e inferencia hacen lazy-load. Es
          +encial para entornos con cómputo limitado.                    
      131 +- **Servicio de inferencia FastAPI** — request/response tipado
          +s con Pydantic, documentación OpenAPI en `/docs`, sondas healt
          +h/ready, carga perezosa del modelo.                           
      132 +- **Integración Optuna** — búsqueda de hiperparámetros con pru
          +ning por trial y unión discriminada `OptunaSpec` (categorical 
          +/ int / float / log) parseada desde `models.toml`.            
      133 +- **Pydantic v2 en toda la base** — modelos de dominio (`Drug`
          +, `Variant`, `StarAllele`, `Genotype`, `PredictionRequest`, …)
          + en cada frontera; configuración vía `pydantic-settings`.     
      134 +- **Pipeline NGS** — FastQC → BWA-MEM → Picard → Freebayes → V
          +EP, sin `shell=True`, subprocesos sanos.                      
      135  
       84 -- **Arquitectura Híbrida:** Modelo DeepFM con _Embeddings_ din
          -ámicos y _Attention Mechanism_ para capturar interacciones com
          -plejas fármaco-gen.                                           
       85 -- **Inferencia Flexible:**                                    
       86 -  - Modo Interactivo (CLI) para consultas rápidas.            
       87 -  - Modo _Batch_ para procesar grandes volúmenes de pacientes 
          -(CSV/TSV).                                                    
       88 -- **Optimización Automatizada:** Integración nativa con **Optu
          -na** para la búsqueda de hiperparámetros.                     
       89 -- **Configuración Modular:** Sistema basado en archivos TOML (
          -`models.toml`, `config.toml`) que permite definir nuevas arqui
          -tecturas sin tocar el código.                                 
       90 -- **Pipeline Bioinformático:** Procesamiento de secuenciacione
          -s genómicas a archivos VCF y mapeo a códigos ATC para la predi
          -cción.                                                        
       91 -
